## Assertion and tooling debt

Strengthen required regressions atomically with their associated fixes.

- `full-loop.spec.ts`: require victory, intended nonzero loot, successful equip
  and legal allocation; remove optional paths/swallowed failures.
- `save-load.spec.ts`: remove mobile exclusion; replace allocation ≥0 with a
  known successful allocation and exact persistence.
- `no-gold-currency.spec.ts`: require victory/reward summary before currency
  assertions; verify no-gold runtime/data identities separately.
- `merc-fights.spec.ts`: require an actual merc action/contribution.
- `post-victory-cta.spec.ts`: require victory before victory controls.
- `loot-drop-rate.spec.ts`: boosted fixtures prove transport/eligibility only.
- Resolved tooling defect: `test:related` now uses `vitest related --run` for
  locked Vitest 1.6.1. Use `--project=engine` for a surgical engine loop; broad
  dependency fanout can exceed the related-test budget (BUG-003).

TD approved this four-path protocol/fixture slice before the separate access slice.
Installation receipts now contain immutable logical-wave settlement metadata in
the existing holder. New runs/waves receive fresh opaque payout identities; replay

inherits its exact identity/progress. Next wave requires the exact awaitingNextWave

total metadata; incomplete playback is a no-op. It latches unsettled to settling
before granting effect permission. Playback-mutated teams/log/cursor/pause/speed
are excluded from completion equality. Finish records terminal victory/defeat or
derives the next index and captures already computed carry once, without freezing
caller units/arrays. Fail retains the first arbitrary thrown value and original
recording, with no payout retry/compensation. Only owned wrappers are frozen.
Pending replay blocks all settlement operations, preventing progress forks.

this protocol. Production producers and completion still use their prior path;
settlement access refusals are NOT integrated by this slice.

Combat helpers now resolve the captured live hero-bound instruction, project the
selected item alone and stage immutable Iron input before any write. Qualification
from filtered slots locks replay independently of item, mana, life/full/terminal
success. A successful matching owner/item/canonical-skill/positive-finite-MP and

means no item payment. Exact prewrite revalidation precedes the sole inventory
setter, held under the existing item-debit guard. Unrelated containers/items,
currencies and actual concurrent raw writes are never compensated or overwritten.

After success or arbitrary setter throw, actual live ownership determines unpaid,
committed or conflict. Verified live-original ownership cancels only the unpaid
preparation; its notifying error is wrapped privately with an own exact cause,
including undefined, and the screen returns false for explicit Retry. Direct
callers receive that documented wrapper. A silent unchanged setter refuses.
Committed/conflicting errors retain their exact original values and receipt;
publication recovery never pays again. Before any attempted write, stale staging
cancels using the known unpaid capture. Postwrite cancellation uses only verified
live-original references. The narrow expectedBackpack-as-Item[] assertion keeps
exact frozen-array proof and ordinary immutable inventory replacement semantics.

Locked replay reinstalls the exact original input, recording and publication
without resolution, simulation, selection consumption or debit. It gets a fresh
receipt with null attestation/debit and shares logical payout identity. Unlocked
replay retains ordinary current-configuration behavior but suppresses canonical
and alias Iron slots/item input until the next new wave. Fresh new waves use
carried mana and require a new explicit selection; no automatic source choice.
SaveCurrent11, engine formulas/data and source modules outside helpers are unchanged.
